FINANCIAL REVIEW                                                                                             財務回顧
Revenue                                                                                                      收益
Our Group’s revenue was primarily generated from (i) property management and                                 本集團的收益主要來自(i)物業管理及增值
value-added services; and (ii) property engineering services.                                                服務；及(ii)物業工程服務。

For the Year, our Group’s total revenue was approximately RMB410.6 million,                                  於本年度，本集團收益總額約為人民幣
representing an increase of approximately RMB51.5 million or 14.3% as compared                               410.6百萬元，較截至二零二四年十二月
to approximately RMB359.1 million for the year ended 31 December 2024.                                       三十一日止年度約人民幣359.1百萬元增
Such increase was primarily attributable to the increase in segment revenue from                             加約人民幣51.5百萬元或14.3%。有關增
property management services and property engineering services.                                              加主要由於物業管理服務及物業工程服
                                                                                                             務的分部收益有所增加。

The following table sets out the breakdown of our revenue by type of services                                下表載列各年度按服務類別劃分的收益
during the years:                                                                                            明細：

DIRECTORS’ INTEREST IN A COMPETING BUSINESS                                               董事於競爭業務之權益
In order to avoid any future competition with our Group, on 18 February 2020,             為避免日後與本集團出現任何競爭，於
the controlling shareholders, Ms. Zhang, Foison Amber Development, Glory                  二零二零年二月十八日，控股股東、

deed of non-competition (the “Deed of Non-Competition”) in favour of our                  股東 」）以本公司及其附屬公司為受益人
Company and its subsidiaries. Pursuant to the Deed of Non-Competition, each of            與 本 公 司 訂 立 不 競 爭 契 據（「 不 競 爭 契
the Controlling Shareholders has undertaken and covenanted with the Company               據 」）。根據不競爭契據，各控股股東已
that, she/it shall not, and shall procure that her/its close associates and entities or   與本公司承諾及訂下契諾，於受限制期
companies controlled by her/it (other than a member of the Group) not to, at              間的任何時間，其將不會，並促使其緊
any time during the restricted period, among other things, directly or indirectly,        密聯繫人及受其控制之實體或公司（本集
either on her/its own account or in conjunction with or on behalf of any person,          團成員公司除外）不會（其中包括）自行或
firm or company (in each case whether as a shareholder, partner, agent, employee          連同或代表任何人士、商號或公司（在
or otherwise) carry on, engage, participate, be concerned or interested in or in          各情況下不論是否為股東、合夥人、代

RISK MANAGEMENT AND INTERNAL CONTROL                                                          風險管理及內部監控
The Board recognises its overall responsibilities for maintaining sound and effective         董事會全面負責維持本集團健全及有效
risk management and internal control systems including conducting an annual                   之風險管理及內部監控制度，包括對其
review on their effectiveness for achieving long-term sustainable development of              有效性作出年度審閱，以助本集團達致
our Group. The risk management and internal control systems, under a defined                  長期持續發展。於界定管理架構及其權
management structure with limits of authority, are designed for our Group to                  限下，風險管理及內部監控制度旨在協
identify and manage the significant risks to pursue its business objectives, safeguard        助本集團識別及管理於實現業務目標所
its assets against unauthorised use or disposition, enhance effectiveness and                 面對之重大風險、保障資產免於未經授
efficiency of its operations, ensure the maintenance of proper accounting records             權挪用或處置、提高營運成效和效率、
for reliable financial reporting, and ensure compliance with relevant laws and                確保維護妥當之會計記錄以作出可靠財
regulations. Such systems are designed to manage our risk exposures, primarily                務申報，以及確保遵守相關法律法規。
our operational risk, legal risk and liquidity risk, rather than eliminate risks of failure   該等系統旨在管理我們的風險，主要為
in the achievement of our Group’s business objectives and can only provide                    經營風險、法律風險及流動資金風險，
reasonable, but not absolute assurance against material misstatement or loss.                 而非消除未能實現本集團業務目標之風
                                                                                              險，並僅可就避免重大錯誤陳述或損失
                                                                                              提供合理但並非絕對的保證。

The Board is responsible for the determination of our Group’s risk profile within             董事會負責為本集團就其業務營運上的
its acceptable tolerance levels in business operation, oversight of management                可承受程度而確立風險組合、監督管
in the design, implementation and monitoring of overall risk management                       理層從風險識別、風險評估、制訂合適
process from risk identification, risk assessment, establishment of appropriate risk          的風險應對措施至定期風險檢討及監察
responses and regular risk evaluation and monitoring, so as to ensure the systems             的整體風險管理流程的設計、實施及監
are effectively established and maintained.                                                   察，以確保有關系統可有效地制訂及維

      asset.                                                                                本集團會繼續確認該金融資產。

     Measurement of ECL                                                                  預期信貸虧損之計量
     ECL is a probability-weighted estimate of credit losses (i.e. the present value     預期信貸虧損是金融工具預期年期
     of all cash shortfalls) over the expected life of the financial instrument.         內的信貸虧損（即所有現金短缺的現
                                                                                         值）的概率加權估計。

     For financial assets, a credit loss is the present value of the difference          就金融資產而言，信貸虧損為根據
     between the contractual cash flows that are due to an entity under the              合約結欠實體的合約現金流量與實
     contract and the cash flows that the entity expects to receive.                     體預期收取的現金流量之間的差額
                                                                                         之現值。

     Lifetime ECL represents the ECL that will result from all possible default          全期預期信貸虧損指在金融工具預
     events over the expected life of a financial instrument while 12-month              期年期內所有可能發生的違約事件
     ECL represents the portion of lifetime ECL that is expected to result from          將導致的預期信貸虧損，而12個月
     default events on a financial instrument that are possible within 12 months         預期信貸虧損則指於報告日期後12
     after the reporting date.                                                           個月內可能發生的金融工具違約事
                                                                                         件預期會造成的全期預期信貸虧損
                                                                                         部分。

     Where ECL is measured on a collective basis, the financial instruments              預期信貸虧損按集體基準計量時，
     are grouped based on the following one or more shared credit risk                   金融工具會按以下一項或多項共有
     characteristics:                                                                    信貸風險特徵予以分類：

     (i)     past due information                                                        (i)     過往逾期資料

     (ii)    nature of instrument                                                        (ii)    工具性質

     (iii)   nature of collateral                                                        (iii)   抵押品性質

     (iv)    industry of debtors                                                         (iv)    債務人所在行業

     (v)     geographical location of debtors                                            (v)     債務人的地理位置

     (vi)    external credit risk ratings                                                (vi)    外部信貸風險評級

     Assessment of significant increase in credit risk                                    信貸風險顯著增加之評估
     In assessing whether the credit risk on a financial instrument has increased         於評估金融工具的信貸風險自初始
     significantly since initial recognition, the Group compares the risk of a            確認以來有否顯著增加時，本集團
     default occurring on the financial instrument as at the reporting date with          會比較金融工具於報告日期的違約
     the risk of a default occurring on the financial instrument as at the date of        風險與金融工具於初始確認日期的
     initial recognition. In making this assessment, the Group considers both             違約風險。於作出此評估時，本集
     quantitative and qualitative information that is reasonable and supportable,         團會考慮合理及有據的定量及定性
     including historical experience and forward-looking information that is              資料，包括過往經驗及無需過多成
     available without undue cost or effort. In particular, the following information     本或努力即可獲得的前瞻性資料。
     is taken into account in the assessment:                                             具體而言，於評估中會考慮以下資
                                                                                          料：

     •      the debtor’s failure to make payments of principal or interest on the         •   債務人未能於到期日支付本金
            due dates;                                                                        或利息；

     •      an actual or expected significant deterioration in the financial              •   金融工具之外部或內部信貸評
            instrument’s external or internal credit rating (if available);                   級（如可得）實際或預期顯著惡
                                                                                              化；

     •      an actual or expected significant deterioration in the operating results      •   債務人之經營業績實際或預期
            of the debtor; and                                                                顯著惡化；及

     •      actual or expected changes in the technological, market, economic or          •   技術、市場、經濟或法律環境
            legal environment that have or may have a significant adverse effect              實際或預期發生變化，而已經
            on the debtor’s ability to meet its obligation to the Group.                      或可能對債務人履行對本集團
                                                                                              之責任之能力造成重大不利影
                                                                                              響。

     Irrespective of the outcome of the above assessment, the Group presumes              不論上述評估結果如何，倘合約付
     that the credit risk on a financial instrument has increased significantly since     款逾期超過30日，本集團均會假定

     due.                                                                                 來已顯著增加。

      Low credit risk                                                                      低信貸風險
      A financial instrument is determined to have low credit risk if:                     在下列情況下，金融工具會被認為
                                                                                           具有較低的信貸風險：

      (i)     it has a low risk of default;                                                (i)     其違約風險較低；

      (ii)    the borrower has a strong capacity to meet its contractual cash flow         (ii)    借款人有較強的能力履行短期
              obligations in the near term; and                                                    內的合約現金流量責任；及

      (iii)   adverse changes in economic and business conditions in the longer            (iii)   經濟及營商環境的長期不利變
              term may, but will not necessarily, reduce the ability of the borrower               動可能但不一定會降低借款人
              to fulfil its contractual cash flow obligations.                                     履行其合約現金流量責任的能
                                                                                                   力。

      Simplified approach of ECL                                                           預期信貸虧損之簡化方法
      For trade receivables and contract assets without a significant financing            就並無重大融資成分的貿易應收款

      The Group recognises a loss allowance based on lifetime ECL at each                  化方法計算預期信貸虧損。本集團
      reporting date and has established a provision matrix that is based on its           根據各報告日期的全期預期信貸虧
      historical credit loss experience, adjusted for forward-looking factors specific     損確認虧損撥備，且已根據過往信
      to the debtors and the economic environment.                                         貸虧損經驗建立撥備矩陣，並就債

2.   MATERIAL ACCOUNTING POLICIES (Cont’d)                                            2. 重大會計政策（續）
     Financial instruments (Cont’d)                                                     金融工具（續）
     Impairment of financial assets and other items (Cont’d)                            金融資產及其他項目之減值（續）
     Credit-impaired financial asset                                                    信貸減值之金融資產
     A financial asset is credit-impaired when one or more events that have a           當發生一項或多項事件而對金融資
     detrimental impact on the estimated future cash flows of that financial asset      產的估計未來現金流量產生不利影
     have occurred. Evidence that a financial asset is credit-impaired include          響時，該金融資產即已出現信貸減
     observable data about the following events:                                        值。金融資產出現信貸減值的證據
                                                                                        包括有關以下事件的可觀察數據：

     (a)   significant financial difficulty of the issuer or the borrower.              (a)   發行人或借款人面臨重大財政
                                                                                              困難。

     (b)   a breach of contract, such as a default or past due event.                   (b)   違反合約，如違約或逾期事
                                                                                              件。

     (c)   the lender(s) of the borrower, for economic or contractual reasons           (c)   借款人的貸款人出於與借款人
           relating to the borrower’s financial difficulty, having granted to the             財政困難有關的經濟或合約原
           borrower a concession(s) that the lender(s) would not otherwise                    因而向借款人授出貸款人於其
           consider.                                                                          他情況下不會考慮的優惠。

     (d)   it is becoming probable that the borrower will enter bankruptcy or           (d)   借款人有可能破產或進行其他
           other financial reorganisation.                                                    財務重組。

     (e)   the disappearance of an active market for that financial asset because       (e)   因財政困難以致該金融資產的
           of financial difficulties.                                                         活躍市場消失。

     (f)   the purchase or origination of a financial asset at a deep discount that     (f)   以大幅折扣購買或產生金融資
           reflects the incurred credit losses.                                               產，並反映已產生信貸虧損。

     Write-off                                                                          撇銷
     The Group writes off a financial asset when the Group has no reasonable            當無法合理預期可收回金融資產之
     expectations of recovering the contractual cash flows on a financial asset in      全部或部分合約現金流量時，本集
     its entirety or a portion thereof. The Group has a policy of writing off the       團會撇銷金融資產。本集團根據收
     gross carrying amount based on historical experience of recoveries of similar      回類似資產的過往經驗制定撇銷總
     assets. The Group expects no significant recovery from the amount written          賬面值的政策。本集團預期不會大
     off. However, financial assets that are written off could still be subject         幅收回撇銷金額。然而，經考慮法
     to enforcement activities under the Group’s procedures for recovery of             律意見（如適用）後，已撇銷的金融
     amounts due, taking into account legal advice if appropriate. Any subsequent       資產仍可能根據本集團收回逾期款
     recovery is recognised in profit or loss.                                          項的程序被採取強制執行行動。任
                                                                                        何後續收回的款項均會於損益確認。

      Impairment of non-financial assets, other than                                     非金融資產（商譽除外）之減值

      At the end of each reporting period, the Group reviews internal and                於各報告期末，本集團會審閱內外

      is estimated, based on the higher of its fair value less costs of disposal and     倘存在任何該等跡象，則資產之可

      of an individual asset, the Group estimates the recoverable amount of the          本及使用價值兩者之較高者進行估

      generating unit (the “CGU”)).                                                      金額，本集團會估計可獨立產生現
                                                                                         金流量之最小組別資產（即現金產生
                                                                                         單位（「現金產生單位」））之可收回金
                                                                                         額。

      If the recoverable amount of an asset or a CGU is estimated to be less than        倘資產或現金產生單位之估計可收
      its carrying amount, the carrying amount of the asset or CGU is reduced to         回金額少於其賬面值，則將該資產

     Provisions                                                                         撥備
     Provisions are recognised when the Group has a present legal or                    於可能需要就含有經濟利益的資源

     to settle the obligation, and when a reliable estimate of the amount of            而承擔的現有法定或推定責任確認
     obligation can be made. Expenditures for which a provision has been                撥備。因確認撥備產生的開支會於
     recognised are charged against the related provision in the period in which        開支產生期間內的相關撥備中扣
     the expenditures are incurred. Provisions are reviewed at the end of each          除。本集團會於各報告期末檢討及
     reporting period and adjusted to reflect the current best estimate. Where          調整撥備以反映當前最佳估計。倘
     the effect of the time value of money is material, the amount provided is          貨幣的時間價值影響屬重大，則撥
     the present value of the expenditures expected to be required to settle            備金額為預期需用以償付責任的開
     the obligation. Where the Group expects a provision to be reimbursed,              支的現值。倘本集團預計撥備可獲
     the reimbursement is recognised as a separate asset but only when the              償付，則僅於償付款可實質性確定
     reimbursement is virtually certain.                                                時將有關款項確認為獨立資產。

     account and is released to profit or loss over the expected useful life of the     倘補助與一項資產有關，則公平值

      The lease payments are discounted using the interest rate implicit in the           租賃付款使用租賃內隱含的利率折
      lease, or where it is not readily determinable, the incremental borrowing           現，或如無法輕易釐定該利率，則
      rate of the lessee.                                                                 使用承租人之增量借款利率折現。

      Subsequently, the lease liability is measured by increasing the carrying            隨後，租賃負債透過增加賬面值以
      amount to reflect interest on the lease liability and by reducing the carrying      反映租賃負債利息及透過減少賬面
      amount to reflect the lease payments made.                                          值以反映已付租賃付款計量。

      The lease liability is remeasured using a revised discount rate when there          倘租賃付款因租期變動或本集團重
      are changes to the lease payments arising from a change in the lease term           新評估是否合理確定行使購買選擇
      or the reassessment of whether the Group will be reasonably certain to              權而出現變動，則使用經修訂貼現
      exercise a purchase option.                                                         率重新計量租賃負債。

      The lease liability is remeasured by using the original discount rate when          倘餘值擔保、實質固定租賃付款或
      there is a change in the residual value guarantee, the in-substance fixed lease     某一指數或比率（浮動利率除外）出
      payments or the future lease payments resulting from a change in an index           現變化導致未來租賃付款出現變
      or a rate (other than floating interest rate). In case of a change in future        動，則使用原有貼現率重新計量租

                                                                                          債。

      The Group recognises the amount of the remeasurement of the lease                   本集團將租賃負債之重新計量金額

      amount of the remeasurement in profit or loss.                                      於損益確認重新計量之任何餘額。

            registered capital. Subject to certain restrictions set out in the relevant         準則釐定，直至儲備餘額達到
            PRC regulations and in the articles of association of the entities, the             其註冊資本50%為止。受相關

            be converted to increase share capital provided that the balance after              所載若干限制的規限，法定盈

      rates specified in the rules of the scheme.                                       團須按計劃規則指定比率，向基金
                                                                                        支付的供款。

      fully in the contributions, the amount of the forfeited contributions will be     金計劃，則已沒收的供款額將用於
      used to reduce future contributions payable by the Group.                         減少本集團未來應付的供款。

      schemes. The only obligation of the Group with respect to the retirement          款。本集團就退休福利計劃的唯一
      benefit scheme is to make the required contributions under the scheme.            責任為根據計劃作出所需供款。

      (a) Financial risk management objectives and                                    (a) 財務風險管理目標及政策

          (ii)   Credit risk (Cont’d)                                                    (ii) 信貸風險（續）
                 The Group’s customer base consists of a wide range of clients                本集團的客戶群由廣大
                 and the trade receivables and contract assets are categorised                客戶組成以及貿易應收
                 by common risk characteristics that are representative of the                款項及合約資產以共有
                 customers’ abilities to pay all amounts due in accordance                    風險特徵分類，該等特
                 with the contractual terms. The Group applies a simplified                   徵可代表客戶按照合約
                 approach in calculating ECL for trade receivables and contract               條款支付所有到期款項
                 assets and recognises a loss allowance based on lifetime ECL                 的能力。本集團應用簡
                 at each reporting date and has established a provision matrix                化方法計量貿易應收款
                 that is based on its historical credit loss experience, adjusted             項及合約資產的預期信
                 for forward-looking factors specific to the debtors and the                  貸虧損，根據各報告日
                 economic environment. The expected loss rate used in the                     期的全期預期信貸虧損
                 provision matrix is calculated for each category based on                    確認虧損撥備，根據其
                 actual credit loss experience over the past years and adjusted               過往信貸虧損經驗建立

35. CAPITAL MANAGEMENT                                                            35. 資本管理
  The objectives of the Group’s capital management are to safeguard its ability      本集團的資本管理目標為確保其具
  to continue as a going concern in order to provide returns for shareholders        備持續經營的能力，以為股東提供
  and benefits for other stakeholders, to maintain an optimal capital structure      回報及為其他利益相關者帶來收
  to reduce the cost of capital and to support the Group’s stability and             益，維持最佳資本結構以降低資本
  growth. The directors of the Company consider the total equity as disclosed        成本並支持本集團的穩定與增長。
  in the consolidated statement of financial position as the Group’s capital.        本公司董事將綜合財務狀況表中所
                                                                                     披露的權益總額視為本集團的資本。

  The Group actively and regularly reviews and manages its capital structure         經計及本集團的未來資本要求，本
  to ensure optimal capital structure and shareholder returns, taking into           集團主動及定期審閱及管理其資本
  consideration the future capital requirements of the Group. In order to            結構以確保最佳資本結構及股東回
  maintain or adjust the capital structure, the Group may adjust the amount          報。為維持或調整資本結構，本集
  of dividends paid to shareholders, issue new shares or return capital              團可能調整向股東派付的股息金
  to shareholders. No changes were made in the objectives, policies or               額、發行新股份或向股東退還資
  processes during the years ended 31 December 2025 and 2024.                        本。於截至二零二五年及二零二四
                                                                                     年十二月三十一日止年度，並無更
                                                                                     改有關目標、政策或程序。

  The Group monitors capital using a gearing ratio, which is interest-bearing        本集團以資產負債比率（即計息銀行
  bank and other borrowings divided by total equity. As at the end of the            及其他借款除以權益總額）監察其資
  reporting period, there were no interest-bearing bank and other borrowings.        本。於報告期末，並無計息銀行及
  As such, no gearing ratio as at the end of the reporting date was presented.       其他借款。因此，於報告期末並無
